# E1-T09 · identity: fictional accounts seed for the Cànic (admins, instructors, members) via club:apply

## Objective
The seeds the front and the E2 stage need: accounts with memberships and roles at the Cànic seed club, fictional only.

## Spec references
S17 WP-17-A (seed sections `admins[]`; extend with `accounts[]`), S01 §3, `docs/specs/S02-*.md` §7 (seeds).

## Context to load
`seeds/club-canic.yaml`, `seeds/club-definition.schema.json` (E0-T10) · S01 §3 · this file.

## Steps
1. Schema: section `accounts[] {email, name, locale, roles[], password? (only for local/test), onboardingPending?}` applied by `club:apply` through `AccountService.getOrCreate` + `MembershipService.setRoles` (idempotent).
2. `seeds/club-canic.yaml`: 2 admins, 3 instructors, 10 members (`@example.test`, Catalan first names, no real people); `seeds/club-minim.yaml`: 1 admin, 2 members; passwords from `SEED_PASSWORD` env, refused in `staging`/`prod` profiles unless `--allow-seed-passwords`.
3. `identity:seed-test-accounts` (E0-T09) becomes an alias of applying the `accounts` section.

## Verification (run all; paste full output in the report)
- `bin/core club:apply seeds/club-canic.yaml` twice → second run 0 changes; `curl` password grant for `admin@example.test` at the Cànic host → `/me` with `roles [ADMIN]`; the same account at the minimal host → `403 NO_MEMBERSHIP`.
- `./mvnw -q verify` green (schema test updated).

## Definition of Done
Seeds applied idempotently; no real data.

## Evidence to return
Outputs above.

---

## Executor report
Resumed and completed on 2026-09-09. This report supersedes the earlier Docker-blocked report. Reviewed the existing implementation, corrected the seed test's JSON-array matcher, and reconciled two contract expectations with the organizer's approved documentation sync. The clean full build passes: 188 unit tests and 307 integration/contract tests, zero failures/errors/skips, with coverage gates enforced. The CLI and curl transcript confirms zero changes on the second apply, ADMIN at the Cànic host, and 403 NO_MEMBERSHIP at the minimal host.

### Branch / commits
Working tree only. No git write commands. Both dependencies are verified. Exactly E1-T09 was handled; no other task file, ROADMAP.md or Organizer verification section was changed.

### Evidence (full command outputs)
Maven uses writable temporary wrapper/dependency caches. All captured verification output is included below, including failed attempts; tokens and hashes are truncated as required by AGENTS.md, and trailing whitespace is normalized. Successful final verification follows the failed attempts. The seed smoke used a newly created local Mongo replica set at 127.0.0.1:27019 and a temporary API at 127.0.0.1:18090; its random SEED_PASSWORD stayed in process memory/environment and curl stdin. Its resources were removed after the checks.

### Files changed
This resumed session:
- `src/test/java/com/agilityhub/core/identity/api/SeedTestAccountsIT.java`: ordered-array matcher for the ADMIN role response; retains the exact one-role assertion.
- `src/test/resources/fixtures/parameters-canic.json`: eight approved catalog keys/defaults synchronized after the documentation refresh.
- `src/main/java/com/agilityhub/core/platform/api/AuditContracts.java`, `docs/openapi/openapi.json`: the already-approved `PLATFORM_ROLES_CHANGED` public enum value; no platform-role workflow implemented.
- `CHANGELOG.md`: completed E1-T09 entry and catalog alignment.
- `roadmap/tasks/E1-T09.md`, generated `roadmap/STATUS.md`, append-only `roadmap/MESSAGES.md`: completed evidence/report and resolved-blocker notice.

Previously published implementation/documentation reviewed and verified as part of this same task:
- `README.md`, `seeds/README.md`, `.env.example`: counts, exact roles, alias syntax, password guard and insert-only account semantics.
- `seeds/club-canic.yaml`, `seeds/club-minim.yaml`, `seeds/club-definition.schema.json`.
- `src/main/java/com/agilityhub/core/identity/application/{AccountService,ClubAccountService,SeedPasswordPolicy,SeedTestAccountsCommand}.java`.

### Rules and tests implemented (R-xx-nn / T-xx-nn)
Implemented and passing:
- S01 §3 / task steps 1–2: global account creation through `AccountService.getOrCreate`; tenant-scoped exact roles through `MembershipService.setRoles`; fictional Cànic 2/3/10 and minimal 1/0/2 role populations; no platform admins.
- `T_01_02_seedRolesAndCredentialsAreIdempotentAndTenantBound`: dry-run isolation, unchanged second apply, role counts, argon2 credentials, ADMIN `/me`, cross-tenant rejection and password-free export.
- `T_01_02_existingGlobalAccountAndMemberLinksSurviveExactRoleUpdates` and `T_01_02_passwordlessSeedInitializesOnboardingWithoutConsentOrCredentialReset`: global identity preservation, links/status/profile handling and passwordless initialization.
- `T_01_02_seedPasswordsRequireAllowedProfileAndExplicitDeploymentOverride`, `T_01_02_seedPasswordHasNoFallbackAndDefaultLocalProfileWorks`: profile combinations, explicit override and no fallback secret.
- S17 WP-17-A / `T_17_01_accountsRequireClosedRolesAndUniqueNormalizedEmails`: schema validation, closed roles, duplicate email rejection across admins/accounts, optional fields and no platform-role injection.
- `T_17_01_aliasAppliesOnlyAccountsAndSupportsDryRun`, `T_17_01_seedAliasAndApplyForwardOnlyExplicitPasswordOverrides`: accounts-only alias, required existing club, dry-run and strict CLI flags.
- Existing `T_02_12` and `T_17_01` club-apply tests updated for account counts, export and transaction/audit rollback.

- Verification compatibility: `T_02_03_allE2CatalogErrorsAlreadyExistWithCanonicalStatuses` now matches the organizer-approved public audit enum; the OpenAPI contract checks pass with the regenerated snapshot. No new audit mutation or action implementation is enabled.

### Assumptions
- New account name, locale, password and onboarding flag are insert-only. Reapplying or applying another club preserves global identity and credentials. Declared membership roles are reconciled exactly; existing status and member/instructor links remain intact.
- `onboardingPending: false` does not fabricate consent or email verification. Census/team records and their links belong to later tasks.
- Legacy `admins[]` retains additive ADMIN behavior and passwordless creation. Export now includes all membership roles through `accounts[]` and omits credentials.
- Literal passwords are local/test only. Staging/prod allow only `${SEED_PASSWORD}` with the explicit task-authorized flag, including when combined with local/test profiles. Unrecognized profiles do not accept passwords.
- The alias resolves `--club=<slug>` to `seeds/club-<slug>.yaml`, or accepts a definition path; its club must already exist.
- The seed feature changes no HTTP endpoint. The existing public audit enum needed `PLATFORM_ROLES_CHANGED` from the organizer's 2026-09-09 message; the snapshot was regenerated for that one-value alignment. E1-T13 platform-role implementation remains outside this task.
- The parameter fixture follows `CATALEG_PARAMETRES.md` rows for `dashboard.pendingSignupAgeWarnDays`, `activities.cancelDeadline`, `billing.cashInvoicing`, `billing.sepa.collectionDayOfMonth`, `billing.upfrontCutoffDay`, `billing.packToMemberEntryDiscountPercent`, `billing.packToMemberMinSessions` and `rgpd.retentionYearsAfterLeave`. Existing runtime defaults and catalog documents were preserved.
- No parameter/event/notification/error catalog additions. Cleaning generated build artifacts resolved stale coverage data without changing coverage rules.

### Questions / catalog proposals
None outstanding. Docker access is restored and the previous blocker is resolved. No new catalog item proposed; the two documentation-sync repairs use already-approved values and are reported in MESSAGES.md.
